- **studio-lead**: Direttore dello studio {{STUDIO}}. Usalo per qualunque richiesta marketing/creativa che tocchi più di una disciplina, o quando non è chiaro chi debba lavorarci. Does not judge palette; fails a criterion when its PNG shows a different thing than the one named. - **su-reviewer-fable**: 극한(fable) 모드 전용 리뷰 에이전트 (Fable high). Opus 리뷰어 3명과 따로, 전체 관점으로 diff를 검토해 문제를 찾는다. 수정하지 않는다. 훅이 ORCHESTRATE_FABLE=1일 때만 허용한다. fable 스킬의 워크플로에서만 쓴다. ([KJH-code/castaway](https://github.com/KJH-code/castaway/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/su-reviewer-fable.md))
- **su-scout**: 강화·최대·극한(plus·super·fable) 모드 전용 정찰 에이전트 (Sonnet medium). 지정된 영역의 파일·함수·테스트 명령을 찾아 구조화된 목록으로 돌려준다. 수정하지 않는다. plus·super·fable 스킬에서만 쓴다. ([KJH-code/castaway](https://github.com/KJH-code/castaway/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/su-scout.md))
- **su-tester**: 강화·최대·극한(plus·super·fable) 모드 전용 테스트 에이전트. 완료 기준 중 명령으로 확인 가능한 항목만 실행해 통과/실패 표를 만든다. 수정하지 않는다. plus·super·fable 스킬에서만 쓴다. ([KJH-code/castaway](https://github.com/KJH-code/castaway/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/su-tester.md))
- **su-verifier**: 강화·최대·극한(plus·super·fable) 모드 전용 반박 검증 에이전트. 리뷰 지적 하나(또는 같은 파일·영역의 지적 최대 3개)를 받아 반박을 시도하고 지적마다 성립/기각/미확인으로 판정한다. 수정하지 않는다. plus·super·fable 스킬에서만 쓴다. ([KJH-code/castaway](https://github.com/KJH-code/castaway/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/su-verifier.md))
- **sub**: 只在 codex 引擎下，把「输出」里的写法换成带「思路」字段的版本（思路可选，只记录不参与判断）。 ([phoenix-phi0/agent_mcp](https://github.com/phoenix-phi0/agent_mcp/blob/HEAD/tools/dslagent/agents/sub.md))
- **Sub Agents**: Specialized sub-agents for `spawn_subagent`. Each agent is defined by a level-2 heading and a `name: agent` YAML fence directly below it. ([googlicius/obsidian-steward](https://github.com/googlicius/obsidian-steward/blob/HEAD/agents/Sub%20Agents.md) · ★85)
- **sub-agent**: **Type**: Task Execution Agent **Related Persona**: P-007 (Sub-Agent) **Purpose**: Reliable, observable task execution with structured progress reporting **Version**: 1.0.0 ([noizu-labs-ml/NoizuPromptLingo](https://github.com/noizu-labs-ml/NoizuPromptLingo/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/sub-agent.md) · ★13)
- **sub-agent**: Execution worker on a shipd spec-driven build — claims tasks from the coordinator and implements them exactly per the change artifacts, never guessing. ([shipd-now/shipd](https://github.com/shipd-now/shipd/blob/HEAD/plugins/s/agents/sub-agent.md) · ★2)

- **sub-agent-artist**: Art stage for the Z-Day pipeline. Use for any task that creates or changes sprites, tiles, item icons, object sheets, walls, ledges, paths, UI textures or VFX. Works in the task's git worktree, edits the offline generators' models in .claude/tools/art/ (never hand-paints PNGs), regenerates, returns mockups for approval as QUESTION, commits the shipped assets on the task branch, and returns a stage report with handoff notes for the developer. ([spencerlepine/z-day](https://github.com/spencerlepine/z-day/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sub-agent-artist.md))
- **sub-agent-coordinator**: Specialist for coordinating complex sub-agent interactions. MUST orchestrate sub-agent communication protocols. Anti-trigger: do not perform implementation; do not replace sceptic or formal score. ([Timothy191/arch-systems-portal](https://github.com/Timothy191/arch-systems-portal/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/sub-agent-coordinator.md))
- **sub-agent-developer**: Coding stage for the Z-Day pipeline. Use for any feature, bug fix, refactor, tuning, generator config or map regeneration. Works in the task's git worktree given by the orchestrator, wires in assets from earlier artist/sound stages, tests, runs QA and CI there, commits on the task branch, and returns a stage report (usually READY-TO-MERGE with a proposed commit message). Never merges or touches main. ([spencerlepine/z-day](https://github.com/spencerlepine/z-day/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sub-agent-developer.md))
- **sub-agent-maker-agent**: 从重复出现的 human-cc 轨迹中提炼出窄边界 subagent 草稿时使用；只产出 draft，不自行启用。 ([kanydao/ml-template](https://github.com/kanydao/ml-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sub-agent-maker-agent.md) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **sub-agent-merge-conflict-resolver**: Merge-conflict resolver for the Z-Day pipeline. Spawned only by the orchestrator when a task branch, squashed to one commit, conflicts while rebasing onto main, or rebases cleanly but then fails npm run build. Rebases the branch in its worktree, resolves conflicts so both sides keep their intent, regenerates generated content instead of hand-merging it, re-runs the build, and returns RESOLVED, QUESTION or BLOCKED. Never touches main. ([spencerlepine/z-day](https://github.com/spencerlepine/z-day/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sub-agent-merge-conflict-resolver.md))
- **sub-agent-sound-design**: Sound stage for the Z-Day pipeline. Use for any task that adds or replaces sound effects, ambience or music. Works in the task's git worktree, commits on the task branch, and returns a stage report with listen-to notes. ([spencerlepine/z-day](https://github.com/spencerlepine/z-day/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sub-agent-sound-design.md))
- **sub-agent.system**: You are a Novus Agenti / Omni Claw sub-agent, spun up for a single layer of build/review work and then archived. ([c10vis-poem/raw-databank](https://github.com/c10vis-poem/raw-databank/blob/HEAD/horizons-ui-full/agents/sub-agent.system.md))
- **sub-agents**: Dynamic agents are child Durable Objects **colocated under and supervised by** a parent agent, built on the runtime's facet primitive. Each child runs in its **own isolate** with its **own SQLite database**, but lives inside the parent's Durable Object: the parent spawns it, can abort or delete it, and is the only way to reach it. Inside an agent they are typed RPC stubs reached via `this.dynamicA ([cloudflare/agents](https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/sub-agents.md) · ★6k · 5 copies)
- **sub-agents**: The rules are summarized in `backend/lunora/sub-agents/AGENTS.md`, which agents load automatically; this file holds the detail. Paths are relative to `backend/lunora/`. ([anolilab/neore](https://github.com/anolilab/neore/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/sub-agents.md) · ★14)
- **sub-agents**: Sub-agents are isolated processes spawned via the Task tool. They receive NO conversation context and evaluate/perform tasks independently. ([ilandahan/AID](https://github.com/ilandahan/AID/blob/HEAD/rules/agents/sub-agents.md) · ★10)
- **sub-agents**: A **sub-agent** is another agent that your agent hands one self-contained task to, waits for, and gets an answer back from. Use it when a piece of work wants a different role — research, code review, image generation — or when the intermediate steps would flood the main conversation. ([yogasw/wick](https://github.com/yogasw/wick/blob/HEAD/docs/guide/agents/sub-agents.md) · ★4)
- **sub-agents**: Sub-agents are autonomous child sessions spawned by the main agent to handle focused, independent work. Each sub-agent gets its own conversation context, full tool access, and runs to completion before returning results to the parent. ([billionzeros/computer](https://github.com/billionzeros/computer/blob/HEAD/specs/features/agents/sub-agents.md))
- **sub-agents**: Sub-agents are child Durable Objects colocated under a parent agent. Each sub-agent has its own isolated SQLite storage and its own WebSocket connections, but shares the parent's machine and is spawned, listed, and torn down through the parent. Clients reach a sub-agent directly via a nested URL; inside an agent, sub-agents are typed RPC stubs. ([dawszk-hash/agents](https://github.com/dawszk-hash/agents/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/sub-agents.md))

- **SUBAGENT_TO_CLAUDE_AGENT_MAPPING**: **Created**: October 21, 2025 **Purpose**: Map Linear SubAgent roles to available Claude Code specialized agents for task execution ([kingler/v0-jetvision-assistant](https://github.com/kingler/v0-jetvision-assistant/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SUBAGENT_TO_CLAUDE_AGENT_MAPPING.md))
- **SubAgentAnalystComplex**: Analyzes ambiguous or cross-component problems and design trade-offs. May edit documents, not code, tests, or configuration. Not for raw facts or baseline collection. Moderate cost and speed. ([n-r-w/pi-config](https://github.com/n-r-w/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agent-suite/agent-selection/agents/SubAgentAnalystComplex.md))
- **SubAgentAnalystCritical**: Analyzes high-risk decisions where errors have severe impact. May edit documents, not code, tests, or configuration. Not for raw facts or baseline collection. High cost and slow. ([n-r-w/pi-config](https://github.com/n-r-w/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agent-suite/agent-selection/agents/SubAgentAnalystCritical.md))
- **SubAgentAnalystRegular**: Handles bounded analysis and technical judgment. May edit documents, not code, tests, or configuration. Not for raw facts or baseline collection. Very low cost and fast. ([n-r-w/pi-config](https://github.com/n-r-w/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agent-suite/agent-selection/agents/SubAgentAnalystRegular.md))
- **SubAgentCoderComplex**: Makes cross-component code, test, or configuration changes requiring significant design decisions. Not for read-only work or baseline collection. Moderate cost and speed. ([n-r-w/pi-config](https://github.com/n-r-w/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agent-suite/agent-selection/agents/SubAgentCoderComplex.md))
- **SubAgentCoderCritical**: Makes high-risk code, test, or configuration changes where errors have severe impact. Not for read-only work or baseline collection. High cost and slow. ([n-r-w/pi-config](https://github.com/n-r-w/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agent-suite/agent-selection/agents/SubAgentCoderCritical.md))
- **SubAgentCoderRegular**: Makes routine code, test, or configuration changes with bounded design decisions. Not for read-only work or baseline collection. Very low cost and fast. ([n-r-w/pi-config](https://github.com/n-r-w/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agent-suite/agent-selection/agents/SubAgentCoderRegular.md))
- **SubAgentCoderSimple**: Makes small, obvious code, test, or configuration changes without design decisions. Not for read-only work or baseline collection. Extremely low cost and super fast. ([n-r-w/pi-config](https://github.com/n-r-w/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agent-suite/agent-selection/agents/SubAgentCoderSimple.md))
- **SubAgentCodeSimplificationFinder**: Finds opportunities to simplify and improve code for clarity, consistency, and maintainability while preserving exact functionality. No code modification ([n-r-w/pi-config](https://github.com/n-r-w/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agent-suite/agent-selection/agents/SubAgentCodeSimplificationFinder.md))
- **SubAgentCodeSlopFinder**: Responsible for finding code slop in codebase. No code modification ([n-r-w/pi-config](https://github.com/n-r-w/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agent-suite/agent-selection/agents/SubAgentCodeSlopFinder.md))
- **SubAgentCritic**: Critical analysis of proposed solution from real development perspective, identifying non-hypothetical issues. Use when you need to stress-test a plan, implementation, or design. SubAgentCritic does NOT REPLACE REVIEW, it is only for FINAL "grilling" of results. High cost and slower speed. ([n-r-w/pi-config](https://github.com/n-r-w/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agent-suite/agent-selection/agents/SubAgentCritic.md))
- **subagente-accesibilidad-ux**: Revisor de accesibilidad, usabilidad técnica y consistencia de interacción para componentes y demoapp. ([janpereira-dev/skillsCurate](https://github.com/janpereira-dev/skillsCurate/blob/HEAD/.agents/agent-hub/imports/skillsCurate/.agents/agents/subagente-accesibilidad-ux.md) · 2 copies)
- **subagente-adaptador-ebook**: Subagente autônomo para manufatura completa de 1 E-book em paralelo — adapta o tom dos capítulos já escritos do livro-mãe (sem pesquisar de novo) e gera o arquivo EPUB final. ([Heverton-web/skill-manager](https://github.com/Heverton-web/skill-manager/blob/HEAD/fabrica-de-livros/.claude/agents/subagente-adaptador-ebook.md) · 3 copies)
- **subagente-adaptar-datos-a-la-plantilla-que-te-exigen**: Úsalo cuando el usuario deba entregar datos en la plantilla exigida por otro (carga de productos en un marketplace, hoja de cálculo de un cliente, formato de importación de una plataforma) y su archivo de origen tenga columnas, formatos o códigos distintos. El agente construye una correspondencia de campos documentada, transforma los datos, los valida y genera un lote de prueba. Úsalo antes de cualquier importación masiva. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/documentos-datos-hojas/agents/subagente-adaptar-datos-a-la-plantilla-que-te-exigen.md))
- **subagente-analista-de-pliegos-y-bases-de-concurso**: Úsalo cuando el usuario reciba un documento largo de licitación, una convocatoria de subvenciones o las bases de un concurso y necesite un informe de decisión de una página: requisitos de elegibilidad, documentación exigida, criterios de puntuación con puntos, plazos, límites de formato y riesgos de exclusión. Úsalo antes de decidir si presentarse y de nuevo antes de entregar. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/documentos-revision-docs/agents/subagente-analista-de-pliegos-y-bases-de-concurso.md))
- **subagente-angular-arquitectura**: Especialista en arquitectura Angular, contratos públicos, diseño de componentes y patrones mantenibles para librerías. ([janpereira-dev/skillsCurate](https://github.com/janpereira-dev/skillsCurate/blob/HEAD/.agents/agent-hub/imports/skillsCurate/.agents/agents/subagente-angular-arquitectura.md) · 2 copies)
- **subagente-archivador-y-renombrador-de-ficheros**: Úsalo cuando el usuario tenga una carpeta desordenada de documentos (propuestas, facturas, entregables, escaneados) con nombres incoherentes y duplicados, y quiera una convención de nombres coherente, una estructura de carpetas, detección de duplicados y un índice. Úsalo para una limpieza puntual o como puesta a punto periódica. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/documentos-pdf-archivo/agents/subagente-archivador-y-renombrador-de-ficheros.md))
- **subagente-arte-final**: Subagente especializado na síntese global da obra concluída e geração da Capa, Contracapa, diagrama animado do ecossistema e conceito visual premium da capa via MCP image_gen e skills de design auxiliares. ([Heverton-web/proj_livros](https://github.com/Heverton-web/proj_livros/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/subagente-arte-final.md))
- **subagente-auditor-de-plantilla-de-marca-en-documentos**: Úsalo cuando los documentos de Word o PowerPoint del usuario se vean incoherentes y necesite una auditoría frente a su plantilla de marca: fuentes, colores, estilos de título, márgenes, numeración de páginas, índice, resolución de imágenes y pie legal. Úsalo antes de enviar un documento importante o al estandarizar un conjunto de archivos. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/documentos-revision-docs/agents/subagente-auditor-de-plantilla-de-marca-en-documentos.md))
- **subagente-calidad-estandares**: Revisor de lint, seguridad, naming, deuda técnica, estándares y disciplina de ingeniería. - **subagente-cartas-personalizadas-en-masa**: Úsalo cuando el usuario necesite generar muchos documentos personalizados a partir de una plantilla y un archivo de datos (CSV u hoja de cálculo) - cartas, presupuestos, certificados, avisos de cambio de precios, facturas - con validación de los datos antes de generarlos y un archivo de salida por cada fila. Úsalo para combinaciones de correspondencia de cualquier tamaño. - **subagente-comparador-de-versiones-de-contrato**: Úsalo cuando el usuario reciba una versión revisada de un contrato, propuesta o documento largo y necesite una lista clara y priorizada de qué cambió entre las dos versiones, separando las ediciones cosméticas de los cambios con consecuencias reales. Úsalo antes de firmar o aceptar un documento devuelto. - **subagente-ui**: Subagente Diseñador Visual de UI (Planificador Visual). Diseña el sistema estático de tokens (paleta Dark Luxury OLED, escala modular, espaciados en cuadrícula de 4pt), la jerarquía visual y la maquetación en StyleSheet nativo sin modificar código lógico. ([IRRT3000/IAN-APp](https://github.com/IRRT3000/IAN-APp/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/subagente-ui.md) · ★2)
- **subagente-ui**: Diseñador visual de UI en solo lectura (tokens Dark Luxury OLED, escala 4pt, StyleSheet nativo). Respaldo cuando Antigravity no está disponible: entrega el documento de diseño de una pantalla o componente. ([IRRT3000/IAN-APp](https://github.com/IRRT3000/IAN-APp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/subagente-ui.md) · ★2)
- **subagente-verificador-de-cifras-antes-de-enviar**: Úsalo cuando un documento esté escrito y a punto de enviarse (presupuesto, propuesta, informe, contrato) y necesite una comprobación final de coherencia: totales de tablas, IVA y porcentajes, lógica de fechas, referencias cruzadas a anexos y apartados, moneda y unidades, y marcadores de posición olvidados. Úsalo como último paso antes de enviar. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/documentos-revision-docs/agents/subagente-verificador-de-cifras-antes-de-enviar.md))
- **subagentes-ejemplo**: Los subagentes de Claude Code son archivos `.md` en `.claude/agents/` con un frontmatter y unas instrucciones. ([justindurruae-coder/Claude-Lyceum](https://github.com/justindurruae-coder/Claude-Lyceum/blob/HEAD/agents/subagentes-ejemplo.md))
- **SubAgentExtractor**: Collects facts from sources and commands, including baseline lint, test, and build checks. No analysis, judgment, review, or implementation. Lowest cost and fastest. ([n-r-w/pi-config](https://github.com/n-r-w/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agent-suite/agent-selection/agents/SubAgentExtractor.md))
- **subagents**: Load this file before dispatching a subagent. Use delegation only when the active harness and task instructions permit it. Tool availability, context inheritance, and model selection come from the harness; repository isolation rules stay in [Version Control](version-control.md). ([hewigovens/jayjay](https://github.com/hewigovens/jayjay/blob/HEAD/agents/subagents.md) · ★229)
- **subagents**: Specialized agents to spawn for scoped work, so verbose output stays out of the main context window and independent tasks run in parallel. Each entry is a ready-to-paste system prompt plus **when to use** and **tool scope**. ([lonormaly/builders-stack](https://github.com/lonormaly/builders-stack/blob/HEAD/agents/subagents.md) · ★42 · 3 copies)
- **subagents**: Read this before touching `SubagentRun`, `SubagentRegistry`, `subagent_spawn`, `agent_spawn`, `AgentRunner.taskFireParent`, a task's model pin, coding runs or `AgentService.delete`. ([tsukhani/jclaw](https://github.com/tsukhani/jclaw/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/subagents.md) · ★42)
- **subagents**: Sub-agents are agents that work under a supervisor agent to handle specific tasks. This architecture allows you to create agent workflows where each sub-agent focuses on a specific domain, coordinated by a supervisor. ([HIDORAKAI002/ai-workspace-archive](https://github.com/HIDORAKAI002/ai-workspace-archive/blob/HEAD/ai_agent_frameworks/multi_agent___specialized/voltagent/website/docs/agents/subagents.md) · ★39 · 2 copies)
- **subagents**: Claude Code subagents of this project live in `.claude/agents/`. They take the work that floods the main session's context (Gradle logs, CodeRabbit threads) or needs a narrow, project-specific reviewer. Every one of them only reports: the main session triages, fixes, talks to the user and runs git, the board and the report. ([IliaVoskoboinikov/Finance-Manager](https://github.com/IliaVoskoboinikov/Finance-Manager/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/subagents.md) · ★24)
